## Idea

An integer `m` is said to **divide** an integer `n` if there exists an integer
`k` equipped with an identification `km ＝ n`. Using the
[Curry–Howard interpretation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Curry–Howard_correspondence)
of logic into type theory, we express divisibility as follows:

```text
  div-ℤ m n := Σ (k : ℤ), k *ℤ m ＝ n.
```

If `n` is a nonzero integer, then `div-ℤ m n` is always a proposition in the
sense that the type `div-ℤ m n` contains at most one element.

We also introduce **unit integers**, which are integers that divide the integer
`1`, and an equivalence relation `sim-unit-ℤ` on the integers in which two
integers `x` and `y` are equivalent if there exists a unit integer `u` such that
`ux ＝ y`. These two concepts help establish further properties of the
divisibility relation on the integers.

### The equivalence relation `sim-unit-ℤ`

We define the equivalence relation `sim-unit-ℤ` in such a way that
`sim-unit-ℤ x y` is always a proposition.

<pre class="Agda"><a id="presim-unit-ℤ"></a><a id="3929" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#3929" class="Function">presim-unit-ℤ</a> <a id="3943" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="3945" href="elementary-number-theory.integers.html#1293" class="Function">ℤ</a> <a id="3947" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="3949" href="elementary-number-theory.integers.html#1293" class="Function">ℤ</a> <a id="3951" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="3953" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="3956" href="Agda.Primitive.html#915" class="Primitive">lzero</a>

<a id="sim-unit-ℤ"></a><a id="4017" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4017" class="Function">sim-unit-ℤ</a> <a id="4028" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="4030" href="elementary-number-theory.integers.html#1293" class="Function">ℤ</a> <a id="4032" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="4034" href="elementary-number-theory.integers.html#1293" class="Function">ℤ</a> <a id="4036" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="4038" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="4041" href="Agda.Primitive.html#915" class="Primitive">lzero</a>
<a id="4047" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4017" class="Function">sim-unit-ℤ</a> <a id="4058" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4058" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="4060" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4060" class="Bound">y</a> <a id="4062" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="4064" href="foundation-core.negation.html#595" class="Function Operator">¬</a> <a id="4066" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="4067" href="elementary-number-theory.integers.html#1605" class="Function">is-zero-ℤ</a> <a id="4077" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4058" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="4079" href="foundation-core.cartesian-product-types.html#585" class="Function Operator">×</a> <a id="4081" href="elementary-number-theory.integers.html#1605" class="Function">is-zero-ℤ</a> <a id="4091" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4060" class="Bound">y</a><a id="4092" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="4094" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="4096" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#3929" class="Function">presim-unit-ℤ</a> <a id="4110" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4058" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="4112" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4060" class="Bound">y</a>

## Properties

### Divisibility by a nonzero integer is a property

<pre class="Agda"><a id="is-prop-div-ℤ"></a><a id="4195" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4195" class="Function">is-prop-div-ℤ</a> <a id="4209" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="4211" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="4212" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4212" class="Bound">d</a> <a id="4214" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4214" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="4216" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="4218" href="elementary-number-theory.integers.html#1293" class="Function">ℤ</a><a id="4219" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="4221" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="4223" href="elementary-number-theory.nonzero-integers.html#834" class="Function">is-nonzero-ℤ</a> <a id="4236" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4212" class="Bound">d</a> <a id="4238" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="4240" href="foundation-core.propositions.html#1029" class="Function">is-prop</a> <a id="4248" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="4249" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#2357" class="Function">div-ℤ</a> <a id="4255" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4212" class="Bound">d</a> <a id="4257" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4214" class="Bound">x</a><a id="4258" class="Symbol">)</a>
<a id="4260" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4195" class="Function">is-prop-div-ℤ</a> <a id="4274" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4274" class="Bound">d</a> <a id="4276" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4276" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="4278" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4278" class="Bound">f</a> <a id="4280" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="4282" href="foundation-core.propositional-maps.html#2396" class="Function">is-prop-map-is-emb</a> <a id="4301" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="4302" href="elementary-number-theory.multiplication-integers.html#19624" class="Function">is-emb-right-mul-ℤ</a> <a id="4321" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4274" class="Bound">d</a> <a id="4323" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4278" class="Bound">f</a><a id="4324" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="4326" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#4276" class="Bound">x</a>
</pre>
### The divisibility relation is a preorder

Note that the divisibility relation on the integers is not antisymmetric.

### If `k` divides `x` and `k` is `0` then `x` is `0`

<pre class="Agda"><a id="is-zero-is-zero-div-ℤ"></a><a id="5683" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#5683" class="Function">is-zero-is-zero-div-ℤ</a> <a id="5705" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="5707" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="5708" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#5708" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="5710" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#5710" class="Bound">k</a> <a id="5712" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="5714" href="elementary-number-theory.integers.html#1293" class="Function">ℤ</a><a id="5715" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="5717" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="5719" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#2357" class="Function">div-ℤ</a> <a id="5725" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#5710" class="Bound">k</a> <a id="5727" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#5708" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="5729" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="5731" href="elementary-number-theory.integers.html#1605" class="Function">is-zero-ℤ</a> <a id="5741" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#5710" class="Bound">k</a> <a id="5743" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="5745" href="elementary-number-theory.integers.html#1605" class="Function">is-zero-ℤ</a> <a id="5755" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#5708" class="Bound">x</a>

### An integer is a unit if and only if it is `1` or `-1`

### If `sim-unit-ℤ x y` holds and both `x` and `y` have the same sign, then `x ＝ y`

<pre class="Agda"><a id="eq-sim-unit-is-nonnegative-ℤ"></a><a id="21495" href="elementary-number-theory.divisibility-integers.html#21495" class="Function">eq-sim-unit-is-nonnegative-ℤ</a> <a id="21524" class="Symbol">:</a>
